info.ice.desc	"冰能够起到保持凉爽的作用，有着许多工业用途。\n\n20世纪初期，一场寒冷革命来袭。哪怕到了现在，想要一次性获得大量冰块，也只能通过结冰的湖与河流获取，然后将其储存在地下。整个过程不仅昂贵，而且难以管理。但随着电制冷的出现，冰成为了一种可以在工厂中生产的东西。冰在制造多种奢侈的酒水和<sprite name=""res apple-jack"">烈性苹果酒时非常有用。你可以在特定商家购买冰；如果你需要更稳定的材料供给，可以自己建造一个冰库。\n\n<indent=15><b>基础价格\n<sprite name=""ui buy""> $2/盒</b></indent>\n\n<i><indent=30><i>文明起源于蒸馏。</i></indent>\n<indent=40>—威廉·福克纳</indent>"	"Ice keeps things cool and has many industrial uses\n\nThe early 20th century brought about a cold revolution. Until recently, the only way to get large quantities of ice was from frozen lakes and rivers. Then stored underground, it was a fairly expensive and difficult to manage resource. But with the advent of electrical refrigeration, ice become something else that could be churned out of factories. Ice is useful in making many types of luxury booze and for making <sprite name=""res apple-jack""> applejack. A crew member that has gained the appropriate experience managing this operation will allow you to upgrade it to an Improved Malt Processor.\n\n<i><indent=30>A brewer can make very good beer from indifferent malt, but his efforts, if carried back to the malt-house, are much more certain and reliable in their effects.</i>\n</indent><indent=40>—Henry Stopes</indent>"

info.industrial-filters.name	工业过滤器	Industrial Filters
info.industrial-filters.desc	处理原材料会累积大量废料残渣，尤其是在发酵和蒸馏的过程中，这些不必要的产物会影响到私酒业务的最终产品质量。为应对这一问题，你学习了这个技能，可以在业务中添加<b>工业过滤器</b>升级措施，提高业务从每一批原材料中获得的产量。\n\n<indent=30><i>没有工业的生活是罪恶，没有艺术的工业是野蛮。</i></indent>\n<indent=40>—约翰·罗斯金</indent>	"Unwanted debris, particles left over from processing raw materials and other residues that accumulate during the processing of raw materials prior to fermentation and distillation can have an impact of the final product of your booze operations. To counter that, once you've learned this skill, you can add the <b>Industrial Filters</b> expansion to your operations to increase the yield from each batch of raw materials.\n\n<indent=30><i>Life without industry is guilt, and industry without art is brutality.</i></indent>\n<indent=40>—John Ruskin</indent>"
info.good-yeast.icon	"<sprite name=""skill expansions"">"	"<sprite name=""skill expansions"">"
info.good-yeast.name	高质量酵母	High Quality Yeast
info.good-yeast.desc	酵母，工业产出的真菌，是发酵的引擎。它们能够将植物中的糖变成酒精，也因而成为了人类最喜欢的微生物。顶级工业酵母能够以更快的速度完成自己的工作。如果你能弄到一些，就可以在自己的私酒生产业务中添加<b>高质量酵母</b>升级措施，加快生产速度。\n\n<indent=30><i>随着我对自然的研究，我愈发感叹于造物主的鬼斧神工。在实验室工作时，我会进行祈祷。</i></indent>\n<indent=40>—路易斯·巴斯德</indent>	"Yeast - those industrious little fungi - are the engines of fermentation. They're ability to produce alcohol from simple plant sugars has made them our species' most favored microbe. Good top-quality industrial grade yeast does its job much faster, so if you get your hands on that, you can install a <b>High Quality Yeast</b> expansion in your booze production operations to make them run faster.\n\n<indent=30><i>The more I study nature, the more I stand amazed at the work of the Creator. I pray while I am engaged at my work in the laboratory.</i></indent>\n<indent=40>—Louis Pasteur</indent>"

info.trap-door.name	活板门	Trap Doors
info.trap-door.desc	禁酒令的出现为酒保们出了一个难题，他们既要拥有一定数量的私酒，满足顾客的需要，这个数量还不能过高，不然就会引来警方的大规模突袭。他们想到的答案就是——活板门。学习此技能后，你就可以在自己的地下酒吧和非法酒吧中安装<b>活板门</b>，提高每天的私酒销售量。\n\n<indent=30><i>没有什么比秘密传播得更快。</i></indent>\n<indent=40>—利蒂希亚·伊丽莎白·兰登</indent>	"Prohibition presents barkeeps with a conundrum - the need to keep sufficient booze on hand for customers but not so much that in the event of a raid, the cops abscond with an undue amount. The answer? Secret doors. Learning this skill allows you to install <b>Trap Doors</b> in your bootleggers and speakeasies to increase the amount of booze they can sell each day.\n\n<indent=30><i>Nothing circulates so rapidly as a secret.</i></indent>\n<indent=40>—Letitia Elizabeth Landon</indent>"

info.protection.name	保护费勒索	Protection Rackets

info.hostility.name	敌对与冲突	Hostility & Conflict
info.hostility.desc	"当其他人认为，他们对于你和你的帮派的厌恶已经达到了必须做出行动的程度时。\n\n<sprite name=""ui goon defiant""><link=""goons"">本地流氓</link>或<link=""rivals"">敌对帮派</link>会想出许多理由，将彼此的不信任上升为公开冲突。\n\n对于本地流氓而言，这意味着如果你的团伙成员与他们控制的<sprite name=""ui corner""> <link=""corners"">街角</link>距离过近，他们便会发起攻击并一路追击到数个街角之外。如果你曾与他们控制的街角上的<link=""businesses"">商家</link>存在生意往来，那么这项往来将会中止，直至流氓停止对本地商人的骚扰。\n\n与本地流氓发生冲突的触发条件包括，在他们控制的街角上停留过长时间、对他们的家族成员做出了某种敌对行为、或直接向他们发起攻击。另外，一些本地流氓对出现在附近的新面孔十分敏感，可能会立即与你的帮派敌对。但一切都会在他们习惯了你的出现后回归于常态。\n\n当敌对帮派与你敌对时，他们的做法更加多样。他们有时会召集人手，向你的一名团伙成员发起<sprite name=""ui combat""> 攻击。如果该帮派装备精良，那么成为他们目标的团伙成员就会迎来非常不幸的下场，甚至会导致<sprite name=""ui dead crew""> 死亡。\n\n他们可能会以你的<sprite name=""ui pip territory expand""> <link=""fronts"">门面</link> 作为目标，说服其中的商家业主<sprite name=""ui player lost front""> 将门面关闭。\n\n你<sprite name=""ui pip social expansion""> <link=""controlled-buildings"">控制的建筑</link>会变得容易受到攻击和损害，在完成修理前无法正常运作。\n\n他们还可能开始对<sprite name=""ui corner raided"">帮派附近的街角发动突袭，令那里的商家在一段时间内无法与你进行任何交易，由于这些商家会开始抱怨与你产生联系所导致的惨剧，公众的不满情绪也会因而增加。\n\n无论哪种情况，都会导致<sprite name=""ui heat""> <link=""heat"">热度</link>的大幅增加，进而引来<sprite name=""police station""> <link=""police"">本地警察</link>的更多注视。\n\n不仅会令你损失大量钱财，还会对你帮派的根基造成动摇。处理本地流氓时，你可以简单地要求他们停手。只要你的帮派拥有更多的人力和武器，他们会退回自己的街角，从此互不干涉。\n\n处理敌对帮派时，你需要向他们请求<sprite name=""ui truce started""> <link=""truce"">和解</link>，暂时搁置彼此的敌对关系。"	"When others decide that their dislike for your outfit requires action.\n\nThere could be many reasons that a <sprite name=""ui goon defiant""> <link=""goons"">local hooligan</link> or <link=""rivals"">rival outfit</link> has decided to escalate things from mutual distrust into open conflict.\n\nFor local hooligans, this means that they'll attack your crew members if they get too close to their controlled <sprite name=""ui corner""> <link=""corners"">corner</link> and they may chase after your crew members for a few corners. If you previously had a deal with them to transact commerce with <link=""businesses"">businesses</link> on their corner, that arrangement will end as will any deal that was made where they agreed to stop bothering local merchants. \n\nSome triggers for conflict with local hooligans include spending too much time on their corner, doing something hostile to one of their family members, or attacking them directly. Also, some local hooligans are just touchy about newcomers and may be immediately hostile toward your outfit. But that will eventually pass as they become accustomed to your presence.\n\nWhen a rival outfit is hostile toward you, they have a whole range of options. They will sometimes gather their crew and target one of your crew members for <sprite name=""ui combat""> attack. If they're well-armed, that could be quite unfortunate for that crew member and at worst could result in their <sprite name=""ui dead crew""> death.\n\nThey may target your <sprite name=""ui pip territory expand""> <link=""fronts"">fronts</link> and convince the business owner that it's best that they <sprite name=""ui player lost front""> close down the front.\n\nYour <sprite name=""ui pip social expansion""> <link=""controlled-buildings"">controlled buildings</link> will be vulnerable to <sprite name=""ui ai building attack""> attack and damage, leaving them unable to function until repaired.\n\nThey may start <sprite name=""ui corner raided""> raiding corners near your outfit, causing the businesses there to stop buying and selling with you for a time and causing some social unpleasantness as they report the negative results of being associated with your outfit.\n\nIn either case, you should expect a lot of <sprite name=""ui heat""> <link=""heat"">heat</link> and the resulting increase in attention from the <sprite name=""police station""> <link=""police"">local cops</link>.\n\nThis can all get very expensive and make your outfit's existence even more precarious. For local hooligans, you can simply demand that they stop. If your outfit has more manpower and weaponry than they do, they're likely to slink back to their corner and leave you alone.\n\nFor rival outfits, you need to go ask them for a <sprite name=""ui truce started""> <link=""truce"">truce</link> which will put a pause on hostility for a while. "
info.truce.icon	"<sprite name=""ui truce started"">"	"<sprite name=""ui truce started"">"
info.truce.name	和解	Truces
info.truce.desc	"有时冲突在所难免，会对生意产生不利影响。\n\n如果你与一个<link=""rivals"">敌对帮派</link>正处于<sprite name=""ui ai aggro""> <link=""hostility"">对立</link>状态，你可以主动请求和解或接受对方的和解请求。如果你主动发起请求，那么对方可能会向你索要赔偿，用来降低敌意。\n\n但是，如果敌对帮派主动接近你，并要求你与他们的一名团伙成员在中立地区会面，这就意味着他们已经认定，这场冲突再进行下去对他们不利。在这种情况下，你的帮派就会成为收到补偿的一方。\n\n当和解达成后，在和解生效期间，敌对帮派不会进行任何挑衅行为。如果你决定向已经达成和解的敌对帮派采取敌对行为，就会立刻遭到对方猛烈且迅速的反击。"	"Sometimes conflict is necessary. Sometimes it's just bad for business.\n\nIf you're engaged in <sprite name=""ui ai aggro""> <link=""hostility"">hostilities</link> with a <link=""rivals"">rival outfit</link>, you can request a truce from them or they may request one from you. If you request it, it's likely that the other outfit will want you to pay for it, so they'll demand some amount of cash to cease hostilities.\n\nIf, however, the rival outfit approaches you and asks you to meet one of their crew on neutral ground, that means that they've decided the current conflict is not going their way. In this case, your outfit will be the ones receiving the cash to end the unpleasantness.\n\nOnce a truce has commenced, the rival outfit will cease all provocative action for the duration of the truce. If, however, you decide to take hostile action against a rival with whom you have a truce, you should expect a full and immediate resumption of hostile action."
info.relationships.icon	"<sprite name=""ui pip social"">"	"<sprite name=""ui pip social"">"
info.relationships.name	关系与信任	Relationships & Trust
info.relationships.desc	"在城市的地下世界，认识什么人和知道什么人可以信任对帮派能否取得成功来说至关重要。\n\n你的关系得分总结了你与另一个人的交往历史。它会受到直接正面交互影响，例如对其要价较低或为其完成任务；自然也会受到负面直接交互影响，例如要求支付<sprite name=""ui tribute""> <link=""protection"">保护费</link>或未能帮助他们阻止<sprite name=""ui goon defiant""> <link=""goons"">本地流氓</link>对他们生意的破坏。\n\n关系还会受到个人要素影响，如果你们的<sprite name=""ui ethnicity general""> <link=""ethnicity"">民族</link>相同，就能获得更高的关系得分和信任。另外，有的人天生多疑，就会导致他们对你的信任降低。\n\n这类影响会在你的社交关系中传播。如果你帮助某人完成了一个任务，就会令他的整个家族与你产生关系增益。与之类似，如果你打碎了某人的窗户，他们也会告诉其他人，你是一个不值得信任的人，因而会对关系造成负面影响。\n\n当信任达到一定程度后，人们就会开始与你讨论他们有意开展的非法业务。如果他们对你足够信任，就会愿意为你提供<sprite name=""ui tickets""> <link=""favors"">帮助</link>，例如引荐、传授技能等，对你有益无害。\n\n所以要时刻谨记：你的行动，无论正面还是负面，都会对你的社交关系产生影响。"	"In the city's underworld, who you know and who to trust are critically important to your outfit's success.\n\nYour relationship score summarizes your history with another person. It is influenced by direct positive interactions like cutting someone a break on prices or completing a mission for them. Or those direct interactions can be negative like demanding that they pay you <sprite name=""ui tribute""> <link=""protection"">protection money</link> or failing to help them keep a <sprite name=""ui goon defiant""> <link=""goons"">hostile local hooligan</link> from vandalizing their business.\n\nRelationships can also be influenced by personality features - like if you're both the same <sprite name=""ui ethnicity general""> <link=""ethnicity"">ethnicity</link>, that will result in a higher relationship score and greater trust. On the other hand, they could be cautious by nature and that would lead them to trust you less right away.\n\nBut consequences also spread socially. If you help someone by completing a mission, that results in a positive relationship boost with their entire family. Likewise, if you break someone's windows, they're likely to inform everyone they know that you're not to be trusted, so expect negative relationship impacts.\n\nOnce someone trusts you enough, they will reveal what illegal activities they might want to discuss with you. If they trust you considerably, they will offer you <sprite name=""ui tickets""> <link=""favors"">favors</link> such as introductions, skills and lots of other extremely helpful benefits.\n\nSo keep in mind, your actions, both positive and negative resonate socially."
info.missions.icon	"<sprite name=""ui quests"">"	"<sprite name=""ui quests"">"
info.missions.name	任务	Missions
info.missions.desc	"商家业主有时会想到一些能够为你提供帮助的主意，或者本身需要的帮助，或者对你有所请求。\n\n在城中闲逛时，你可能会看到<sprite name=""ui pip quest available""> 指示，意味着<link=""businesses"">商家业主</link>可能想和你聊聊。当你的组织朝着各个方向发展壮大时，城中与你有着规律贸易往来的人会继续与你的帮派保持来往。例如，他们可能会偶尔向你展示一些新机会。这些机会能够提高你的产量，令你学习一个新<sprite name=""ui skills""> <link=""skills"">技能</link>，使你发现一种先前未知的<link=""resources"">资源</link>、使你获得武器或<link=""vehicles"">车辆</link>、获得<sprite name=""ui tickets""> <link=""favors"">人情</link>或者提高其他物质收益，助你征服城市的罪恶一面。\n\n有的任务必须在特定日期前完成，有的则没有太多限制，可以在你的帮派做好准备后完成。\n\n随着<sprite name=""ui pip territory expand""> <link=""territory"">领地</link>和<sprite name=""ui crew list""> <link=""crew"">团伙</link>的增长，你能够同时进行的任务数量也会随之提升。市民们仅会在你能够接受新任务的情况下给予你任务。所以在完成任务后，在城中四处转转，看看有没有什么新任务可以完成。\n\n时刻寻找新的可完成任务，这些任务就是能够为你提供帮助的机会。如果你帮助了某人，他们就会将你的善举广而告之，令你获得更多机会。与之类似，如果你同意伸出援手却未能兑现承诺，你就会在一些社交圈中成为不值得信任的存在。"	"Business owners will sometimes have some ideas to help you out, need help themselves or have a request for you.\n\nWhile driving around the city, you may see an <sprite name=""ui pip quest available""> indication that a <link=""businesses"">business owner</link> might want to have a word with you. As your organization grows and expands in different directions, the people in the city with whom you regularly transact commerce will keep up with your outfit's comings and goings. As such, they may sometimes have opportunities to present to you. These could increase your productivity, they might result in learning about a new <sprite name=""ui skills""> <link=""skills"">skill</link>, discovering a previously unknown <link=""resources"">resources</link>, gaining weaponry or <link=""vehicles"">vehicles</link>, adding <sprite name=""ui tickets""> <link=""favors"">favors</link> or otherwise materially advancing your efforts to conquer the city's crime landscape.\n\nSometimes the requirements of these missions may need to be fulfilled by a specific date, but sometimes they're more open-ended and can be completed when your outfit is ready. \n\nAs your <sprite name=""ui pip territory expand""> <link=""territory"">territory</link> and <sprite name=""ui crew list""> <link=""crew"">crew</link> grow, so will the number of missions that you're able to have concurrently active. Citizens will only offer you new missions if you're able to accept them, so once one is completed, look around the city to see if any new ones have popped up.\n\nKeep your crew on the lookout for new missions—they can be useful opportunities. If you help someone out, they'll tell others about your stand-up actions which could further broaden your opportunities. Likewise, if you agree to help and go back on your word, you may become known as untrustworthy in certain circles."
info.vehicles.name	车辆	Vehicles
info.vehicles.desc	"在二十世纪二十年代的繁忙城市森林中，想要将你的团伙成员和货物从一处运到另一处……车辆必不可少。\n\n车辆分为两大类——<sprite name=""ui car""> 轿车和<sprite name=""ui truck""> 运送用车。轿车更易于获得和保养。它们价格便宜，在垃圾场、车库和商人那里均可获得。运送用车需要特定<sprite name=""ui skills""> <link=""skills"">技能</link>才能驾驶，用途更加专一。\n\n你可以通过控制<sprite name=""ui pip social expansion""> <link=""buildings"">新建筑</link>，在控制建筑的后方建造一间车库，或帮助<link=""businesses"">商家业主</link>完成<sprite name=""ui quests""> <link=""missions"">任务</link>来增加自己能够拥有的车辆数量上限。\n\n驾驶车辆在城中移动时，你的车辆会承受损耗，所以要积极寻找车库或其他能够修理车辆的商家，也可以建造自己的车辆基础设施。"	"In the bustling urban jungle these booming 1920s cities, a vehicle is necessary to move your crew and your goods from point A to point B. And C. And D...\n\nVehicles come in two broad categories - <sprite name=""ui car""> passenger vehicles and <sprite name=""ui truck""> delivery vehicles. Passenger vehicles are easier to come by and maintain. They're cheap and readily available from dealers, junkyards and garages around town. Delivery vehicles require some <sprite name=""ui skills""> <link=""skills"">skill</link> to properly operate and some more dedicated infrastructure.\n\nYou can increase your capacity for vehicles by gaining control of <sprite name=""ui pip social expansion""> <link=""buildings"">a new building</link>, building a garage of your own in the back of a controlled building or as a reward for helping some <link=""businesses"">business owners</link> who have given you <sprite name=""ui quests""> <link=""missions"">missions</link> to complete.\n\nAs you drive around the city, your vehicles will slowly wear down, so be on the lookout for garages or other businesses who can repair them or build your own vehicle infrastructure."

tut.newgamepopup.text2	今年是1920年，镀金时代的开始。这个国家虽然繁荣，但是分裂。禁酒法令将在国内出售、生产、运输酒类的行为定性为违法行为。这则法令是对那些不支持节制运动的居民和移民们的一次打击。当然，城中没有人会认真对待这则法令：非法私酒售卖和私酒生产业务随处可见，警察也被轻易“说服”，选择对它们视而不见。	"The year is 1920, dawn of the Gilded Age. The country is prosperous but divided. The National Prohibition Act just made it illegal to sell, manufacture, or transport alcoholic beverages anywhere in the country. The law is seen as an attack on city residents and immigrants who don't support the temperance movement. Naturally, nobody in the city takes this law seriously: illegal speakeasies and alcohol operations are popping up all over, and police is easily persuaded to look the other way."

You're staying with your aunt, <b>{name}.</b> She has a small business in the city, and she's going to take you under her wing."
tut.newgamepopup.text4	但你不想在这个家族小店里工作。你想要功成名就。而对于你这样不畏风险的人而言，现在这个时代充满了机遇。	"But you don't want to run the family store. You dream about making it big, and this situation is full of opportunities for someone like you: someone who's not afraid to take risks."
tut.newgamepopup.text5	当你走下火车，摩天大楼与川流不息的人群便映入眼帘……你知道，这是一段伟大故事的开始。	"As you get off the train, and take in the sights of skyscrapers and endless crowds, you already know: this is the beginning of something great."

tut.lesson-2.title	你是谁？	Who Are You?
tut.lesson-2.1	游戏地图上的蓝色标记代表了<b>你</b>，它会跟随着你的汽车移动。\n点击选中。	"This blue marker represents <b>you</b> on the game map, and it will follow your car as you drive around.\nClick on it to select it."
tut.lesson-2.2	看到标记上方那个亮橘黄色光柱了吗？它代表了你当前已被<b>选中</b>，可进行多种操作。	"Do you see the bright, orange column of light above your marker? That shows you that you're currently <b>selected</b> to perform various actions."
tut.lesson-2.3	你可以在右侧用户界面上看到各种关于所选人物的信息。\n我们来逐个查看。	You can see all the information about the selected person on the right side of the UI.\nLet's examine it in order.
tut.lesson-2.4	第一个是人物面部照片，与标记上的相同，很容易辨认。	"First is the person's mugshot, same as on the marker, so it's easy to identify who's who."
tut.lesson-2.5	第二个是人物正在驾驶的车辆，如果被分配了车辆的话。	"Second is the vehicle they're driving, assuming they're assigned to one."
tut.lesson-2.6	"右侧是你的<sprite name=""ui action pts"">行动点数，决定了你每回合能够做些什么，还有……"	"Right next to that are your <sprite name=""ui action pts""> action points, which determine how much you can do per turn, and..."
tut.lesson-2.7	"还有<sprite name=""ui movement pts"">移动点数，决定了你每回合能够移动多远。\n你在驾驶和行动时需消耗上述点数。"	"... and <sprite name=""ui movement pts""> movement points, which determine how far you can drive per turn.\nYou use those up, as you drive around and get things done."
tut.lesson-2.8	如果这个任务被分配了一辆车，你就能在右下方看到车辆的物品栏。	"If this person is assigned to a car, you will see its inventory right below."
tut.lesson-2.9	而在人物的肖像下方，你可以看到该人物的特性和过往经历详情。\n将你的鼠标移到这些条目上就可以查看详情。去试试吧！	"Meanwhile, right below the person's portrait, you can see their traits and details of their past experience.\nMoving your mouse over any of these elements will reveal more details. Go ahead, try it!"
tut.lesson-2.10	最后，选择人物有三种方式：点击人物的标记（在车辆上方），点击团伙卡片（在屏幕的右上角），按下数字键<b>1</b>选择第一个团伙成员。\n按下一次数字键为选择团伙成员，快速按下两次可以将视角平移到上方。\n试试吧！按下数字键<b>1</b>或使用其他方法。	"Finally, to select somebody, you have three options: you can click on their marker (above the car), or click on their crew card (in the upper right corner of your screen), or press the number <b>1</b> key to select the first crew member.\nPressing a number key once will select the crew member, while a quick double-press will also pan the camera over.\nTry it out now! Press the number <b>1</b> key, or use either of the other methods."
tut.lesson-3.title	车辆、现金和其他	"Cars, Cash, and More"
tut.lesson-3.1	现在来看看你的现金和车辆。	Now let's look at your cash and vehicles.
tut.lesson-3.2	你可以在这里看到自己的<b>金钱。</b>这是所有建筑和车辆中的金钱数量总和。\n将你的鼠标移动到这里，查看你在每个地方具体拥有多少钱，还能看到自己在上个回合获得或失去了多少钱。	"Up here you can see your <b>cash.</b> This is the total stored in all your buildings and vehicles.\nMove your mouse over it, to see how much you have in which location - and also, how much you've gained or lost last turn."
tut.lesson-3.3	"再来看看你的<b>车辆</b>：<sprite name=""ui car""> 汽车和 <sprite name=""ui truck"">卡车。每个条目后面显示了拥有的具体数量，以及现在能够拥有的最大数量。\n你需要停车空间或修理店来提高自己能够拥有的最大数量。"	"Next are your <b>vehicles</b>: <sprite name=""ui car""> cars and <sprite name=""ui truck""> trucks. Next to each you have the number owned, and the max number you can handle at the moment.\nYou will need parking spaces or repair shops to increase the max number."

tut.lesson-3.5	左侧的按钮代表了各种信息栏和对话。这些我们之后再聊。	These buttons on the far left show a variety of overlays and informational dialogs. We'll come back to them later.

tut.lesson-3.8	"如你所见，日期前进到了{date}。\n每个回合开始时，你的所有<sprite name=""ui action pts"">行动点数和<sprite name=""ui movement pts"">移动点数都会得到恢复。"	"As you can see, the date advanced to {date}.\nOn each turn, all of your <sprite name=""ui action pts""> action points and <sprite name=""ui movement pts""> movement points get refilled as well."
tut.lesson-4.title	安全屋	The Safehouse
tut.lesson-4.1	现在，这个建筑将会成为你的<b>安全屋。</b>我们进入到里面看看。\n点击标记，打开建筑信息对话框。	Now this building here is going to be your <b>safehouse.</b> Let's take a look inside.\nClick on the marker to open the building info dialog.
tut.lesson-4.2.m	从外面看，这是一个非常普通的商家，由我，你的叔叔{fr}经营。\n但里面空间很大，足够<b>开始一项新业务</b>。	"From the outside this looks like a regular business, operated by myself, your uncle {fr}.\nBut inside there's plenty of storage, and room to <b>start a new operation</b>."
tut.lesson-4.2.f	从外面看，这是一个非常普通的商家，由我，你的婶婶{fr}经营。\n但里面空间很大，足够<b>开始一项新业务</b>。	"From the outside this looks like a regular business, operated by myself, your aunt {fr}.\nBut inside there's plenty of storage, and room to <b>start a new operation</b>."
tut.lesson-4.3	我们依次看看。首先，点击这个<b>生意选项卡。</b>\n就是对话窗口旁边的蓝色选项卡。	"Let's look at this in order. First, click on this <b>business tab.</b>\nIt's a blue tab on the side of the dialog window."
tut.lesson-4.4	我一直在经营的是合法生意。它能够为你想要开展的业务提供良好掩护。	This is the legitimate business that I've been running. It will provide good cover for whatever operation you'll want to set up.
tut.lesson-4.5	现在点击<b>库存选项卡。</b>	Now click on the <b>storage tab.</b>
tut.lesson-4.6	这个面板显示了储存在地下室中的物品。这里存放了许多在禁酒令颁布前生产的酒水！\n我们应该能好好利用一下。	"This panel shows what's stored in the basement. There's a lot of booze here, dating from before Prohibition!\nWe'll make good use of it in a bit."
tut.lesson-4.7	现在点击<b>后室选项卡。</b>	Now click on the <b>back room tab.</b>
tut.lesson-4.8	这个面板显示了建筑后方的情况。\n虽然现在处于空闲状态，但你可以开始一项新业务。	"This panel shows what's set up in the back of the building.\nRight now it's empty, but you could start a new operation."
tut.lesson-4.9	现在点击<b>街角选项卡。</b>	Now click on the <b>corner tab.</b>
tut.lesson-4.10	这个对话显示了我们所在的<b>街角</b>的具体情况。\n街角是领地的基础单位，每个建筑都属于一个街角，而街角可能是他人领地的一部分。	"This dialog shows information about the <b>corner</b> we're on.\nA corner is the basic unit of territory - every building belongs to some corner or another, and corners may belong to someone's territory."
tut.lesson-4.11	这些是存在于这个街角上的商家。\n只要你来到这个街角，就能对这些商家进行拜访。	"These are the businesses at this corner.\nWhenever you're at some corner, you can visit all the businesses there."
tut.lesson-4.12	这里显示了你在这个街角的<b>威望等级</b>，以及附近其他知名人物的威望等级。\n因为你的威望等级非常高（归功于我在人们面前对你的夸赞），这个街角成为了你的领地的一部分。	"This shows your <b>respect level</b> at this corner, and the respect of other people who are known around here.\nBecause you have very high respect level (thanks to me telling people about you), this corner became part of your territory."
tut.lesson-4.13	而在反方向，显示着这个街角的<b>热度等级</b>。将鼠标移动到它上面了解更多详情。\n非法活动会产生热度，而热度会引来警方的注意。	"Conversely, this is the <b>heat level</b> at this corner. Mouse over it to get more details.\nIllegal activity generates heat, and heat attracts police attention."
tut.lesson-4.14	说曹操，曹操就到，这里是与我们所在<b>警区</b>有关的信息。将鼠标移动到这里了解更多详情。\n目前为止一切平静，平静有利于生意的发展。	"Speaking of the devil, here is information about the <b>police precinct</b> we're in. Mouse over it to get more details.\nSo far, it's been quiet. And quiet is good for business."
tut.lesson-4.15	好吧，先把它关掉，回到我们的安全屋。	"Ok, let's close this now and get back to our safehouse."
tut.lesson-4.16	现在，点击我肖像上的选项卡。	Now click on the tab with my portrait.
tut.lesson-4.17.m	这是我，你亲爱的叔叔{fr}。我之前答应了你的父母，要带你来这座城市闯荡一番。	"This is me, your dear uncle {fr}. I told your parents I'll take you in and help you get started in the city."
tut.lesson-4.17.f	这是我，你亲爱的婶婶{fr}。我之前答应了你的父母，要带你来这座城市闯荡一番。	"This is me, your dear aunt {fr}. I told your parents I'll take you in and help you get started in the city."
tut.lesson-4.18	先关闭这个对话。	Close this dialog for now.
tut.lesson-4.19	我已经唠叨了有一段时间了。我们出去走走吧。	I've been beating my gums for a while now. Let's go for a walk instead.
tut.lesson-5.title	进行拜访	Paying a Visit
tut.lesson-5.1	看到这边这个商家了吗？它与我们位于同一个街角。我们去拜访一下吧。\n点击标记，对这里的业主进行拜访。	See this business here? It's on the same corner as we are. Let's pay them a visit.\nClick on the marker to visit the owner.
tut.lesson-5.2	我们正在拜访{name}，{bizname}的经营者。\n我们稍后会去聊上一会，但我们要先加深对他们的了解。	"We are now visiting {name}, who runs {bizname}.\nWe'll go have a chat in one second, but first let's find out more about them."
tut.lesson-5.3	将你的鼠标移动到肖像上，你就能了解各种与之相关的详情。\n特性与性格是有用的情报，它们会对你们之间的交流造成影响。民族同样非常重要，同族的人们总会对你更加友善。	"Move your mouse over the portrait, and you will get a variety of useful details about them.\nTheir traits and temperament are useful to know, they will impact your dealings with them. Ethnicity is also important, as people of the same ethnicity as you will be more friendly towards you."
tut.lesson-5.4	这个计量条显示了人们对你的态度。将鼠标移动到这里，了解更多详情。	"This meter shows how they feel about you. Mouse over it, to get more details."
tut.lesson-5.5	这个人欠你一两次<b>人情</b>！你可以之后再要求其偿还，但需要谨慎选择，毕竟机不可失时不再来。	"This person also owes you a <b>favor</b> or two! You will be able to spend them later - but choose carefully, they don't come often."
tut.lesson-5.6	看看这边。这是我们与人进行友好交谈的方式。\n左侧显示了对方说的话，右侧显示了可选回应。	"Now let's look over here. This is how we can strike up a friendly conversation with this person.\nYou can see what they're saying on the left, and we can choose our response on the right."
tut.lesson-5.7	继续吧，选择第一个选项，询问与买卖有关的消息。	"Go ahead, select the first option to ask about buying and selling."
tut.lesson-5.8	如果你想要买入或卖出某种东西，那就这么做吧。\n但我们还有其他需要查看的事项。	"If you wanted to buy or sell something, this is how you'd go about it.\nBut there's another thing we should check out."
tut.lesson-5.9	我们要查看一下他们都认识哪些人。点击在屏幕左侧高亮显示的人脉按钮。	"Let's see who they know. Click on that connections button, highlighted in the upper left part of your screen."
tut.lesson-5.10	这个对话显示了更多关于此人的详情，其中包括个人信息，以及最重要的，所有现在已知的人脉情况。	"This dialog displays many more details about this person, including personal info and, most importantly, all the connections we know about - which are limited for the time being."
tut.lesson-5.11	人脉对你来说至关重要。\n在眼下的时日，如果你想买入或卖出私酒，就需要认识一些喜欢你、信任你的人。	"Connections are going to be crucial for you.\nIf you're trying to buy or sell any alcohol these days, you gotta know folks who like you, and who trust you."
tut.lesson-5.12	你需要了解如何通过运营这些人脉，达成自己的目的。但这都是后话了。\n现在先关闭这个对话。	"You'll need to learn how to work those connections to get what you need. But, that's for later.\nFor now, close this dialog."
tut.lesson-5.13	更换一个场景如何？我们开车出去转转，你来开车。	How about a change of scenery? Let's go for a ride. You drive.
tut.lesson-6.title	开车转转	Driving Around
tut.lesson-6.1	又一个存在于附近的商家，距离我们一个街角。\n点击标记，查看它的具体情况。	Here's another nearby business. This one is one corner away.\nClick on its marker and let's see what happens.
tut.lesson-6.2	如你所见，虽然显示了一些信息，但我们距离过远，无法进行拜访。\n你需要驾车前往那里才能与商家业主进行对话。	"As you can see, some information is displayed, but we're too far to visit.\nYou will need to drive over there to actually talk to the business owner."
tut.lesson-6.3	这种事你会经常经历：为了完成某件事，你或你的手下必须亲自前往那里，与某人对话。	"This is something you will run into often: in order to get things done, you or one of your people need to actually physically go there and talk to somebody."
tut.lesson-6.4	把车开到那里去吧。点击你的标记，将自己选为当前激活的团伙成员。	So let's drive there. Click on your marker to select yourself as the active crew member.
tut.lesson-6.5	现在<b>右键点击</b>距离这个商家最近的地点。	And now <b>right-click</b> on the location closest to that business.
tut.lesson-6.6	如果你未能到达选中位置，没关系。确保自己处于被选中的状态，然后右键点击你想要前往的位置。	"If you didn't drive to the right place, that's okay. Just make sure you're selected, and then right-click on the location you want to drive to."
tut.lesson-6.7	在驾驶的过程中，你会注意到各个商家的标记颜色不同。\n与你处在同一街角的商家标记为绿色，代表你可以对其进行拜访。\n相反的，距离过远的商家标记为深灰色。	"You can see that markers on businesses change color as you drive.\nThe businesses on the same corner as you turn green, to indicate that you can visit them.\nConversely, the businesses that are too far are dark gray."
tut.lesson-6.8	既然你已经全部掌握，我有一个任务要交给你，你必须自己完成！	"Seeing as this is all copacetic, I have a task for you to try on your own!"
tut.lesson-6.9.m	驾车在附近转转，找到你的表亲{name}。他在附近经营着一家{bizname}。他能够将我们在地下室中存放的部分非法商品售卖出去。\n我不会告诉你{bizname}在哪，因为那是你的第一个任务。那地方就在附近。	Drive around until you find your cousin {name}. He runs {bizname} nearby. He'll be able to liquidate some of the illegal inventory we've got stashed in the basement.\nI won't tell you where {bizname} is located - that's your first task. But it's near here.
tut.lesson-6.9.f	驾车在附近转转，找到你的表亲{name}。她在附近经营着一家{bizname}。她能够将我们在地下室中存放的部分非法商品售卖出去。\n我不会告诉你{bizname}在哪，因为那是你的第一个任务。那地方就在附近。	Drive around until you find your cousin {name}. She runs {bizname} nearby. She'll be able to liquidate some of the illegal inventory we've got stashed in the basement.\nI won't tell you where {bizname} is located - that's your first task. But it's near here.
tut.lesson-6.10.m	找到他，拜访他。这个任务就交给你了。\n记住，如果你的移动点数耗尽了，就点击<b>“下一回合”</b>按钮。	"Find him and pay him a visit. I'll leave you alone for now.\nAnd remember, if you ever run out of movement points, just hit the <b>'Next Turn'</b> button."
tut.lesson-6.10.f	找到她，拜访她。这个任务就交给你了。\n记住，如果你的移动点数耗尽了，就点击<b>“下一回合”</b>按钮。	"Find her and pay her a visit. I'll leave you alone for now.\nAnd remember, if you ever run out of movement points, just hit the <b>'Next Turn'</b> button."

tut.lesson-9.title	探索	Exploration
tut.lesson-9.1	"现在你可以将注意力转移到这里。\n看到这个带有<sprite name=""biz unscoped"">标志的标记了吗？那是一个还未经接触的商家。"	"Now let me direct your attention over here.\nSee this marker with the <sprite name=""biz unscoped""> icon? That's a business that hasn't been scoped out yet."
tut.lesson-9.2	"我们不知道那里是什么情况，但我们能够查明。\n如果你从未到过那里，那就开车前往那个街角，令标记变成绿色吧。\n到达那里后，点击<sprite name=""biz unscoped"">标记进行查探。去试试吧，我等着。"	"We don't know what goes on in there, but we can find out.\nIf you're not there already, drive over to that corner so that the marker turns green.\nOnce you're there, click on the <sprite name=""biz unscoped""> marker to scope it out. Go ahead, I'll wait."
tut.lesson-9.3	"你在街角停留了一段时间，观察着附近的情况，因而消耗了1<sprite name=""ui action pts"">行动点数。\n但现在我们了解了它的名字是<b>{bizname}，</b>我们对于它的具体业务有了一些了解：将鼠标移动到屏幕顶部的通知即可了解全部详情。"	"You took the time to hang out on the corner and observe the comings and goings, so it took 1 <sprite name=""ui action pts""> action point.\nBut now we learned that it's called <b>{bizname},</b> and we got some idea about what it does: you can see all the details if you mouse over the notification at the top of the screen."
tut.lesson-9.4	刚开始探索这座城市时，所有商家都处于未知状态，你可以自由决定对哪个商家进行查探。	"Once you start exploring the city, all businesses will start out unknown, and it's up to you to pick which ones to scope out."
tut.lesson-9.5	说到这个，这是一座巨大的城市，去外面进行一些探索对你来说并无坏处！	"Speaking of which - it's a big city, and it would be good for you to get out there and explore it a little bit!"
tut.lesson-9.6	尚未进行过探索的街角显示为深褐色，你不知道那里都存在哪些商家。\n在对街角进行探索后，你就会发现这里的真实情况。	"Corners that you haven't explored yet show up as sepia colored, and you don't know what kinds of businesses are located there.\nIt's only after you explore a corner that you'll find out what's there."
tut.lesson-9.7	"开车前往这个位置的街角。\n友情提示，该行动会消耗大量<sprite name=""ui movement pts"">移动点数！如果你的移动点数不足，进入下一回合就好。\n前去探索那个新街角吧，我在这里等你。"	"Go ahead and drive to this unknown corner.\nBut just as a heads up - this will consume a lot of <sprite name=""ui movement pts""> movement points! If you don't have enough, don't forget to advance to the next turn.\nGo and explore the new corner, and I'll wait."
tut.lesson-9.8	这就是探索地图的方式——一点一点来、一个街角接着一个街角查探。你可以开着车四处游荡，不断寻找新商家并对它们进行查探，随后与商家业主进行对话，了解他们正在做着什么买卖。\n但如果你能获得暖心的引荐，情况会好上许多，因为对方早已开始信任你了，而获得一个陌生人的信任明显更困难一些。	"So this is how map exploration works - bit by bit, corner by corner, you can drive around, identify new businesses, scope them out, and then talk to the owners to find out what they're buying and selling.\nBut if you can get warm intros, that's much better, because they already start out trusting you. Earning the trust of someone completely unknown is much harder."
tut.lesson-9.9	非常好，你学得很快。\n我还有一个任务要交给你。\n还记得那些存放在安全屋中的啤酒吗？去把它们卖掉吧，全部卖掉！之后我们用得来的钱开展一个新业务。一家酿酒厂如何？或者一家地下酒吧？\n四处看看吧，试着将更多啤酒卖给{name}，或其他对其有购买意愿的人，如果他们愿意将你引荐给其他人，那就接受吧！\n等你总计获得<b>{amt}</b>后再来找我吧，之后我们再聊。	"Great, you're picking things up quickly.\nSo now I have another task for you.\nRemember all the beer stashed in the safehouse? Go ahead and sell it off, all of it! Afterwards we'll invest that money into some kind of a new operation. Maybe a brewery? Or a speakeasy?\nSo go around, try to sell more to {name} or anyone else who's buying, and if they introduce you to more people take them up on it!\nI'll wait until you have <b>{amt}</b> in total, and then we'll continue."
tut.lesson-9.10	你还真是勤勤恳恳啊。\n既然你现在拥有了一笔不小的积蓄，我们可以在这座城市中为我们打造一段新生活了。	"Well aren't you just hitting on all six today.\nNow that you have a nice pile of dough, we can start building a new life for ourselves in this city."
tut.lesson-10.title	业务	Operations
tut.lesson-10.1	现在我们来聊聊<b>业务。</b>想在这座城市里挣大钱，就必须亲自生产并出售非法私酒。\n禁酒法令将酒水的制作和售卖定义为非法——正因如此，这些酒水也变得利润颇丰。\n前提是，你没有被捕。	"Now let's talk about <b>operations.</b> The real money in this city comes from manufacturing and selling your own illegal alcohols.\nThe Prohibition Act made booze making and distribution illegal, and therefore incredibly profitable.\nAs long as you don't get pinched, that is."
tut.lesson-10.2	如要开展一个新业务，你需要控制一栋建筑，且这栋建筑的后面必须拥有足够的可用空间。\n很幸运，这间安全屋后面有着许多空间，这意味着我们可以在这里开展新业务。\n驾车返回安全屋吧。	"To establish a new operation you need a building you control, with some empty space in the back.\nFortunately there's plenty of room in the back of the safehouse, so we can establish a new operation there.\nDrive back to the safehouse."
tut.lesson-10.3	很好，首先，让我们取出一些现金。\n点击安全屋的标记打开安全屋的对话框。然后切换至库存选项卡。	"Good. First, let's unload some of this cash.\nOpen the safehouse dialog by clicking on its marker. Then switch to the storage tab."
tut.lesson-10.4	现在将一批现金从车辆中拿出，放入自己的安全屋。谨记，你可以使用<b>ctrl</b>和<b>shift</b>键以更快的速度移动现金和资源。\n等待你将<b>{amt}</b>储存到安全屋后再来找我吧。	Now move a bunch of cash from your vehicle and into the safehouse. Remember you can use <b>ctrl</b> and <b>shift</b> keys to move cash and resources faster.\nI'll wait here until you have <b>{amt}</b> stored in the safehouse.
tut.lesson-10.5	很好。现在切换至空的后室选项卡。	Great. Now switch to the empty backroom tab.
tut.lesson-10.6	如我之前提到的那样，这间后室是可用的。按下“新业务”按钮。	"As I mentioned, the backroom is available. Press the 'new operation' button."
tut.lesson-10.7	虽然可以开展多种业务，\n但你只能开始那些<i>已知</i>运作方式的业务。在未来，你可以学习到更多业务的运作方式，但现在你能够生产的东西并不多。	"These are the various operations you can set up.\nThese are just the ones you know how to run <i>right now</i>. In the future, you will learn about many more, but right now you only know how to make a few things."
tut.lesson-10.8	既然你认识好几个对啤酒感兴趣的人，那我们就开展<b>自酿啤酒</b>业务吧，这样你就可以轻松地将货物卖出。\n选中该业务后，按下“开展”即可。	"Since you know several people who are interested in beer, let's build the <b>home brew</b> operation, and you'll be able to distribute it easily.\nMake sure that operation is selected, and then press 'Build'."
tut.lesson-10.9	非常好，虽然它需要数个回合才能开始运作，但你可以预览一下它的运作方式。	"Great. It will take a few turns until it's operational. But in the meantime, here's an overview of how this will work."
tut.lesson-10.10	你能够看到，酿酒设备需要<b>消耗</b>麦芽糖浆和空粗陶罐，来<b>生产</b>罐装自酿啤酒。\n将鼠标移动到各个条目，了解更多详情。	"Here you can see the brewery <b>consumes</b> malt syrup and empty stoneware crocks, in order to <b>produce</b> crocks full of homemade beer.\nMove your mouse over the different elements to see more details."
tut.lesson-10.11	另外，你已经拥有了足够的麦芽糖浆储备，但你缺乏足够的粗陶罐！你必须去购买一些，没有足够的材料，这项业务就毫无意义。	"Also, you already have enough malt syrup in storage, but you're missing stoneware crocks! You'll have to go and buy more, because without supplies this operation won't amount to much."
tut.lesson-10.12	另外，当你拥有了更多的金钱和人手之后，就可以考虑为该项业务分配一名管理者，或添加升级措施了，这些都能够极大地提高业务的利润率！	"And by the way, later on when you have more money and people, consider assigning a manager to this operation, or installing some upgrades and expansions. They can make a world of difference in improving your profit margins!"
tut.lesson-10.13.m	真是太棒了，你已经开始了新的酿酒业务，而且几乎没有用到叔叔的帮助。你的父母一定会非常骄傲。这提醒了我，我应该给他们写一封信了。	"Well isn't this the bee's knees, you're already starting a new brewing operation, and with only a little bit of help from your uncle. Your parents would be so proud. Which reminds me, I should write them."
tut.lesson-10.13.f	真是太棒了，你已经开始了新的酿酒业务，而且几乎没有用到婶婶的帮助。你的父母一定会非常骄傲。这提醒了我，我应该给他们写一封信了。	"Well isn't this the bee's knees, you're already starting a new brewing operation, and with only a little bit of help from your aunt. Your parents would be so proud. Which reminds me, I should write them."
tut.lesson-10.14	与此同时，你应该开始生产更多粗陶罐，好让业务可以产出更多商品。\n我不知道有谁在出售粗陶罐，你得自己去寻找。	"In the meantime, you should go and procure more stoneware crocks, so that the operation can start producing some goods for sale.\nI'm not sure who sells them, you'll have to drive around and figure that out yourself."
tut.lesson-11.title	信息栏和报告	Overlays and Reports
tut.lesson-11.1	查看信息栏和报告能够为你提供许多帮助。	"Let's quickly take a look at some informational overlays and reports, which are going to help you in various ways."
tut.lesson-11.2	你会在左上角看到多个下拉菜单，能够为你提供更多信息。\n点击第一个。	In the upper left corner you can see a variety of drop-downs with additional information.\nClick on the first one.
tut.lesson-11.3	这是资源显示，向你展示了你认识的人正在购买或出售哪些特定资源。\n下滑至“粗陶罐”的位置然后点击，查看正在出售这种资源的人物。你还可以查找其他资源。\n你可以按下<b>Z</b>键来快速打开此信息栏。	"This is the resources display. It shows you who you know that buys or sells specific resources.\nGo ahead and scroll down to 'Stoneware Crocks' and click on it, to see who trades in that resource. You can also look through the other resources.\nYou can also bring this overlay up quickly by pressing <b>Z</b>."
tut.lesson-11.4	你也可以选择多种资源，然后点击左侧的高亮按钮将它们标记为喜爱。你每次打开这个信息栏时，这些喜爱的资源都会出现。\n你可以通过点击右侧的高亮按钮来切换喜爱的物品。\n你可以亲自尝试一下。当你完成时，可用同一个左上按钮关闭信息栏，然后我们将继续。	"You can also select multiple resources, and then mark your selection as favorite by pressing the left highlighted button. These favorites will be remembered whenever you open this overlay.\nYou can then toggle your favorites by hitting the right highlighted button.\nFeel free to try it out. When you're done, close the overlay using the same top left button, and we'll continue."
tut.lesson-11.5	资源显示附近是一个信息栏列表。点击将其打开。	Next to it is a list of informational overlays. Click to open it.
tut.lesson-11.6	虽然这里存在多个信息栏，但我们现在只需要关注其中几个。	"There are several overlays here, but we'll focus on just a few right now."
tut.lesson-11.7	<b>威望</b>信息栏显示了你在社区中的威望等级。\n领地的增长基于你在每个街区获得的威望，所以提高威望等级至关重要。这个话题我们之后再聊。\n你可以按下<b>C</b>键来快速打开此信息栏。\n尽情探索吧，准备就绪后点击“继续”即可。	"The <b>Respect</b> overlay shows your level of respect in the neighborhood.\nYour territory grows based on the respect you have on each block, so it's important to grow and keep respect levels high. We'll talk about how to do that a little bit later.\nYou can also bring this report up quickly by pressing <b>C</b>.\nFeel free to explore, and then click 'Continue' when ready."
tut.lesson-11.8	<b>热度</b>信息栏同样非常重要。它显示了警方对每个街角的关注程度，具体程度与非法活动或战斗有关。\n热度等级越高，遭到警方意外“拜访”的几率就越高。\n你可以按下<b>X</b>键来快速打开此信息栏。\n准备就绪后点击“继续”即可。	"The <b>Heat</b> overlay is also very important. It shows the amount of police attention on each corner, such as due to illegal activities or fights.\nThe higher the heat level, the higher the chance of a surprise police visit.\nYou can also bring this report up quickly by pressing <b>X</b>\nClick 'Continue' when ready."
tut.lesson-11.9	<b>警区</b>信息栏显示了每个街角的所属警区。\n每个警区都有一名队长，他们会对区域进行巡逻。与他们建立友谊说不定是个好主意。\n准备就绪后点击“继续”即可。	The <b>Police Precincts</b> overlay shows which corner belongs to which precinct.\nEach precinct has one captain who patrols the area - it might be a good idea to befriend them.\nClick 'Continue' when ready.
tut.lesson-11.10	下方的<b>区域</b>信息栏中显示了城市的区域规划。\n虽然商家通常会出现在工业和商业区中，但居住区中也可能有意外收获。\n准备就绪后点击“继续”即可。	"At the bottom, the <b>Zoning</b> overlays show city zoning regulations.\nBusinesses are typically limited to industrial and commercial zones, but residential zones might have their own surprises too.\nClick 'Continue' when ready."
tut.lesson-11.11	在区域信息栏下方的<b>人口</b>信息栏显示了城中各个民族的分布情况。\n人们会更喜欢与自己民族相同的人，所以你可以利用这一优势。\n准备就绪后点击“继续”即可。	"Below that, the <b>Population</b> overlays show the distribution of different ethnicities in the city.\nPeople of the same ethnicity are predisposed to like each other a bit more than average, so use that to your advantage.\nClick 'Continue' when ready."
tut.lesson-11.12	第三个按钮是下拉菜单，包含了各种报告，按下按钮将其展开。	Now the third drop-down is a collection of reports. Press that button to open it.
tut.lesson-11.13	你可以查看下拉菜单中的多种报告，它们反映着游戏中的各部分内容。\n财政报告可能是其中最吸引人，但其他报告也有各自的用途。\n自由查看一番后，点击“继续”即可。	"This drop-down lists a number of reports you can consult, related to different parts of the game.\nThe financial report is probably the most immediately interesting one, but the others are going to be very useful as well.\nFeel free to look through them, and then click 'Continue'."
tut.lesson-11.14	第四个按钮是游戏的百科全书，里面包含与游戏有关的各种信息。去看看吧！	"Finally the fourth button shows the game encyclopedia, which contains information about various aspects of the game in one place. Take a look!"
tut.lesson-11.15	当你对游戏的某个内容存在疑问时，就来这里看看吧。\n自由查看一番后，点击“继续”即可。	"Whenever you have questions about some game element, check here first.\nFeel free to look around, and then click 'Continue'."
tut.lesson-11.16	好吧，回到收集材料这个目标上来。\n我之后会再来，与你讨论关于领地的话题。发展自己的团伙与领地非常重要，只有这样才能将自己的利益最大化，保护自身不受敌对帮派的攻击。	"All right, I'll let you get back to your goal of gathering supplies.\nI'll be back a little bit later, to talk about territory. Growing your crew and your territory is going to be crucial, in order to maximize profits and also to protect yourself from rival outfits."
tut.lesson-11.17	我刚才提到了敌对帮派，对吧？\n如果目前你还没有树敌，别担心，这是早晚的事。但这个话题我们可以之后再聊。\n现在，我们要将注意力集中在啤酒生产上！完成屏幕左上角的目标，买一些粗陶罐，把他们运送到安全屋。\n顺便，当你开车拜访人们的时候，你可以用<b>左Shift键</b>代替频繁的<b>左键点击</b>以跳过其它话题并直接打开购买或出售对话框。	"Oh yeah, did I say rivals?\nIf you haven't run into any rivals yet, don't worry, you will. But we can talk about that later as well.\nFor now, concentrate on getting some beer production going! Follow the goals in the upper left of your screen, and buy some more stoneware crocks and deliver them to your safehouse.\nBy the way, as you drive around and visit people, instead of a regular <b>left click</b> you can use <b>shift left click</b> to get right into a buying or selling conversation, skipping other topics."
tut.lesson-12.title	门面和领地	Fronts and Territory
tut.lesson-12.1	我们来聊聊另一个重要的话题：<b>领地。</b>\n控制街角是帮派成功的基础。\n拥有自己的领地才能开展更多业务，雇佣更多成员。	Let's talk about another important thing: <b>territory.</b>\nControl of street corners is the cornerstone of every successful outfit.\nYou need to have territory under your control before you can build more operations or hire any crew.
tut.lesson-12.2	现在，你只控制了一个街角，也就是你的安全屋所在的那个。\n但根据我的计算，你还需要控制五个或者六个街角才能开始雇佣更多的人手，扩张自己的帮派。	"Right now you only have one corner under your control, the same one where your safehouse is.\nBut by my calculation, you will need about five or six before you can start hiring more people and growing your outfit."
tut.lesson-12.3	威望是控制街角的基础。你的威望会随着本地人对你了解的加深而提高。威望达到100点后，街角便会归顺于你。	"Control over corners comes from respect. As locals learn about you, your respect rises, and if it's close to 100 the corner will switch over to pay allegiance to you. "
tut.lesson-12.4	领地能够带来很多好处。首先，你拥有的街角越多，能够雇佣的人手、能够控制的建筑数量就越多。\n其次，领地内的商家能够为你提供各种机会。也正因如此，你需要尽可能多地对领地进行扩张。\n最后——或许我不应该告诉你——但你可以向领地内的商家勒索现金。这种做法非常野蛮，可能带来恶劣影响，但却能产生稳定的收入。	"Territory brings many benefits. First, the more corners you have, the more people you can hire and buildings you can control.\nSecond, businesses inside your territory will be offering you all sorts of opportunities, so it's good to expand as much as possible.\nFinally, and maybe I shouldn't be telling you this, but businesses inside your territory can be extorted for cash. It's a brutal thing to do, and it might have repercussions, but it does produce a steady revenue stream."
tut.lesson-12.5	如果要进行勒索，你需要建立一个<b>门面。</b>\n一个新的门面是你在社区内的立足之本，你可以先占领一个街角，然后利用这个街角上的门面将周围所有街角纳入你的领地。\n我会告诉你该怎么做。	"In order to do any of that, you'll need to set up a <b>front.</b>\nA new front is how you get a foothold in a community, by grabbing a hold of a single corner first, and then using that front to bring all the surrounding corners into the fold as well.\nI'll show you how."
tut.lesson-12.6.m	拜访你的表亲{name}，看看他是否有兴趣为你运营门面。	Go visit your cousin {name} and see if he'd be interested in running a front for you.
tut.lesson-12.6.f	拜访你的表亲{name}，看看她是否有兴趣为你运营门面。	Go visit your cousin {name} and see if she'd be interested in running a front for you.
tut.lesson-12.7	"在这场对话中，先进入<sprite name=""ui tickets"">人情步骤，然后询问<sprite name=""ui tribute"">建立一个门面。\n完成对话并支付所需现金和<sprite name=""ui tickets"">人情后即可建立一处门面。"	"In this conversation, start with the <sprite name=""ui tickets""> favors step, and then ask about <sprite name=""ui tribute""> starting a front.\nFinish the conversation and pay the required cash and <sprite name=""ui tickets""> favor to start a front."
tut.lesson-12.8	太好了！一个新门面已经建成。你可以看到，这个街角立刻被纳入了你的领地！	Great! A new front has been set up. You can see this corner immediately joined your territory!
tut.lesson-12.9	门面有多种用途：它们可以用于扩张你的领地，提高你在社区中的威望。如果你正在对各个商家进行勒索，它们还会为你收集保护费。	"Fronts serve multiple purposes: they can be used to expand your territory, by increasing your respect in the neighborhood. Also, if you're extorting businesses, they will be collecting the cash for you to pick up."
tut.lesson-12.10	现在，让这个新门面开始着手提高你在此处的威望吧，使你能够进一步扩张领地。	"Now let's have the new front start work on increasing your respect, to grow the territory even more."
tut.lesson-12.11.m	"回去找你的表亲{name}，这次请求他<sprite name=""ui pip front expansion ready"">扩张你的领地。\n然后同意他开出的价码，这样他就会开始行动。"	"Go back to cousin {name}, but this time ask him about <sprite name=""ui pip front expansion ready""> expanding your territory.\nThen agree to his price, and he'll get started."
tut.lesson-12.11.f	"回去找你的表亲{name}，这次请求她<sprite name=""ui pip front expansion ready"">扩张你的领地。\n然后同意她开出的价码，这样她就会开始行动。"	"Go back to cousin {name}, but this time ask her about <sprite name=""ui pip front expansion ready""> expanding your territory.\nThen agree to her price, and she'll get started."
tut.lesson-12.12	很好，{name}正在努力提高你在这个社区内的知名度。\n在七或八个回合后，这个街角就会认可你并加入你的领地。\n希望你能耐心等待一阵。	"Good, {name} is hard at work improving your name recognition in the neighborhood.\nIn seven or eight turns, that corner should recognize you and join your territory.\nI'll wait with you until it does."
tut.lesson-12.13	<i>（等待新的街角加入玩家的领地，整个过程仅会花费几个回合，你可以在这个过程中打理自己的生意。）</i>	<i>(Waiting for a new corner to join player's territory. It should only take a few turns. Meanwhile you can go about your business.)</i>
tut.lesson-12.14	就是这样！现在你已经控制了三个街角。还不错，甚至可以说非常好。	"And there it is! You now have three corners under your control. Not bad, not bad at all."
tut.lesson-12.15	"现在你要记住，你的门面在为你工作期间，必然会产生一定的费用。所以你每个月至少要去拜访一次自己的门面，承担它们的支出（如果你逾期未到，门面会显示这个<sprite name=""ui pip front needs"">支援图标）。\n另外，你还可以对区域内的商家进行勒索。这个门面会帮你收取保护费，并从中拿出一部分用于承担支出，将剩余的以现金的形式交到你的手上。\n过一段时间之后我们再来。"	"Now here's something to remember. If your front is working on your behalf, it's going to have some costs. Make sure to visit them at least once a month, and cover their expenses (they will display this <sprite name=""ui pip front needs""> support icon if you're overdue).\nAlternatively, you could extort some businesses in the area. Then the front will collect payments, skim off the top to cover expenses, and give you the rest in cold hard cash.\nWe'll come back to this in a moment."
tut.lesson-12.16	继续进行扩张，直至控制的街角总数达到五个。\n你应该回去找{name}，支付一定金钱用于获得更多街角，越多越好。\n另外，你也可以再建造一个门面，但在已经存在的门面附近进行扩张更为划算一些。\n我会帮你进行记录，记得查看屏幕左侧的任务列表。	"Now keep expanding until you've reached five corners total.\nYou should go back to {name} and pay to convert more corners, as many times as possible.\nAlternatively, you could set up a different front, but expanding around an existing front is more cost-efficient in the long term.\nI'll make a note for you, see the missions list on the left side of your screen."
tut.lesson-13.title	勒索	Extortion
tut.lesson-13.1	我之前提到过勒索这个话题，还记得吗？\n我必须承认，自己从来没做过这种事。我是一个心地善良的人，没有人畏惧于我。\n我在自己的小店里总能看到一些暴徒。那些家伙是真正的狠角色，他们会吹嘘自己是如何为附近的商家提供“保护”的。\n我会把方法告诉你。	"I said something about extortion, didn't I?\nI guess it's time for me to fess up. I've never tried something like that myself. And I'm too much of a softie anyway, nobody's afraid of me.\nBut whenever I'm at my local joint, there's a couple of these goons, real tough guy types, boasting how much they're raking in from offering 'protection' to businesses all around.\nLet me tell you how it works."
tut.lesson-13.2	首先，你要找到一个相对弱小的商家。拿这个举例吧。\n它必须处在你的领地内，且必须<i>非常接近门面，</i>否则他们不会畏惧于你，你的计划也无法实施。	"First, you identify a vulnerable business. Like this one, for example.\nIt has to be inside your territory, and it has to be <i>very close to a front,</i> otherwise this won't work, they won't be afraid of you."
tut.lesson-13.3	"驾车前往那里，讨论<sprite name=""ui pip front extortion"">保护相关的事宜。全程保持微笑，但不要表现出友善，明白吗？\n他们会明白你的意图。一些人会立刻屈服，尤其是那些尊重你的人。\n但如果他们拒绝屈服，你就有新麻烦要处理了，而且你必须立刻解决，不然就会有对你不敬的行为产生。"	"Then you drive over there, and strike up a conversation about <sprite name=""ui pip front extortion""> protecting them. Be all smiles, but not friendly, you know?\nThey'll get it immediately. And some of them will cave right away, especially those that really respect you.\nBut if they don't, you'll have a new problem on your hands, and you'll have to do something about it, otherwise you'll have disrespect hanging over you."
tut.lesson-13.4	在任何情况下，勒索都是肮脏的。这会令人们感到不悦，并向其他人抱怨此事。\n与每一个对人造成伤害的行为相同，这个行为也会影响你与其他人的关系。反之亦然，如果你对人们友善，人们也会帮你进行宣扬。	"And in any case, remember that extorting people is a nasty business. It will make them unhappy, and they'll complain to others about it.\nLike every hurtful action, people will talk, and this might hurt your relationships with other people. And vice versa, if you do good by people, they will talk it up as well, and influence others."
tut.lesson-13.5	"我看得出你已经跃跃欲试了。去吧，驾车前往那个商家，试着谈论一下<sprite name=""ui pip front extortion"">“保护”的相关事宜，看看会发生什么事情。我在这里等你。"	"I can see you're itching to give it a try. Go ahead, drive over to that business, try talking about <sprite name=""ui pip front extortion""> protecting them from misfortune, and see what happens. I'll wait here."
tut.lesson-13.6	"<i>（你需要成功地<sprite name=""ui pip front extortion"">在你新获得的领地勒索一个商家）</i>"	"<i>(Waiting for you to successfully <sprite name=""ui pip front extortion""> extort a business inside your newly acquired territory.)</i>"
tut.lesson-13.7.m	你成功地威胁并伤害了一个无辜的人，只为获取一点钱财。\n你可怜的母亲并不知道自己的儿子变成现在这幅样子，真是一件好事。	"And there you go. You threatened and hurt some innocent person, just to make a buck.\nIt's a good thing your poor mother doesn't know how her little boy turned out."
tut.lesson-13.7.f	你成功地威胁并伤害了一个无辜的人，只为获取一点钱财。\n你可怜的母亲并不知道自己的女儿变成现在这幅样子，真是一件好事。	"And there you go. You threatened and hurt some innocent person, just to make a buck.\nIt's a good thing your poor mother doesn't know how her little girl turned out."
tut.lesson-13.8	但这就是弱肉强食的法则。被你勒索的人会每月将一个信封交到距离最近的门面。门面会对保护费进行收集，从中扣除运营费用后，将剩下的保管起来，供你随到随取。\n记得每过一段时间就去你的门面看看，将盈余拿走，或支付必要的运营费用。如果门面破产了，你会失去所有该门面获得的领地！	"But that's how this works. The people you extort will bring a little envelope to the nearest front every month. The front will then collect it all, subtract its operational expenses, and have the rest ready for you to pick up whenever you want.\nJust remember to visit your fronts regularly to pick up the money, or to cover their costs if necessary. If a front goes bankrupt, you will lose all the territory it accumulated!"
tut.lesson-13.9	"现在让我们回到扩张领地这件事上，你现在拥有5个街角。\n首先，去查看门面的业主是否需要<sprite name=""ui pip front needs"">支持或者<sprite name=""ui pip front needs urgent"">紧急支持，并帮助他们。\n然后，与门面业主交谈，并获得更多<sprite name=""ui pip front expansion ready"">街角扩张！\n由于获得5个街角需要一段时间，你可以继续制造并销售自酿啤酒，并继续寻找其他引荐人。晚些再见！"	"And now let's go back to growing your territory, so you have a total of 5 corners.\nFirst, make sure to go and help out the owner of your front if they're in need of <sprite name=""ui pip front needs""> support or <sprite name=""ui pip front needs urgent""> urgent support.\nSecond, talk to the front owner, and get more <sprite name=""ui pip front expansion ready""> corner expansions going!\nAnd since getting to 5 corners is going to take a while, continue to manufacture and sell some homemade beer, and keep looking for more introductions. I'll see you later!"
tut.lesson-14.title	雇佣	Hiring
tut.lesson-14.1	现在你拥有了更大的领地，可以开始考虑增加人手了。\n你所拥有的团伙成员越多，每回合能够进行的事项越多。例如，你可以派遣部分成员进行运送工作，自己则负责处理更重要的生意。	"Now that you have a larger territory, you can start thinking about adding another crew member.\nThe more crew you have, the more you can do per turn. For example, you can send some of your people to run deliveries, while you personally take care of more important business."
tut.lesson-14.2	团伙规模的扩大能够帮助你敌对帮派的进攻，前提是确实有敌对势力存在。	"Having larger crew will also help you defend against rival outfits. Once you run into them, that is."
tut.lesson-14.3	"如要雇佣更多成员，需寻找标记上有<sprite name=""ui pip social crew"">雇佣团伙标志的商家业主。他们会知道一些对工作机会感兴趣的人。\n你需要四处逛逛，通过与他们的交流，了解关于潜在成员的详情。"	"To hire more crew, look for any business owners who have the <sprite name=""ui pip social crew""> hire crew icon on their marker. They know someone interested.\nYou'll need to drive around and talk to them to find out more about that person."
tut.lesson-14.4	"在对话过程中，了解潜在成员的性格特点。这点非常重要，它将决定他们能够胜任的工作类型。\n例如，你不会雇佣一个粗枝大叶的人作为管理者，也不会雇佣一个瘦弱的人作为打手。\n（查看有兴趣加入你方团伙的成员列表，你也可以在<sprite name=""ui info"">报告对话框的<sprite name=""ui crew list"">团伙列表中查看。）"	"As you talk to them, find out about the candidate's personality traits. They're very important, depending on the job.\nFor example, you wouldn't want to hire someone careless to be a manager, or someone weak to be a muscle man.\n(To see a list of everyone who might be interested in joining your crew, you can also open the <sprite name=""ui info""> report dialogs and look at the <sprite name=""ui crew list""> Crew List.)"
tut.lesson-14.5	去试着雇佣一个人。我在这里等你。	Go ahead now and try to hire somebody. I'll wait.
tut.lesson-14.6	<i>（等待你雇佣第二名团伙成员。）</i>	<i>(Waiting for you to hire a second crew member.)</i>
tut.lesson-14.7	很好！你的团伙规模成倍增长了！\n你可以在右侧的列表中查看自己的新团伙成员。	Good! Your crew size just doubled!\nYou can see your new crew member in the list on the right hand side.
tut.lesson-14.8	在常规游戏中，你需要为新雇佣的成员购买一辆新车。\n但在教程中，我为你准备了一辆可以随意使用的旧卡车。\n我把它停在你的安全屋前面了。把它分配给你的新成员吧，我相信你能做到。	"In the course of a regular game, you would also need to buy a new vehicle for this new hire.\nBut in this tutorial, I got a hold of an old truck that you can use.\nI dropped it off in front of your safehouse. Go ahead and assign this vehicle to the new hire. I trust you'll figure out how."
tut.lesson-14.9	<i>（等待你将车辆分配给新成员。）</i>	<i>(Waiting for you to assign the vehicle to the new hire.)</i>
tut.lesson-14.10	就是这样！这名新成员已经做好了工作的准备。\n如果你想手动进行指引，可以点击列表中的人物卡片，告诉他们要去哪里以及要做些什么。你能做的事，他们也都能做。\n另外，你还可以为他们指派一条特定<b>运送路线，</b>他们会按照特定运送列表进行运送。\n我们稍后进行设置。	"And there you go! Now the new member is ready to work.\nIf you want to direct them manually, you can just click on their card in the list, and tell them where to go and what to do. They can do everything that you can.\nAlternatively, you can assign them a specific <b>delivery route,</b> and they will just follow a specific list of deliveries.\nWe’ll get that set up in a moment."
tut.lesson-14.11	完成左侧的任务，到安全屋找我并进行对话。\n我有件事要告诉你。	"Now finish your mission on the left hand side, by visiting me at the safehouse and starting a conversation.\nI have something waiting for you."
tut.lesson-16.title	勇往直前	Moving On
tut.lesson-16.1.m	我亲爱的外甥，我还有最后几条建议要告诉你。	"My dear nephew, I have a few final words of advice for you."
tut.lesson-16.1.f	我亲爱的外甥女，我还有最后几条建议要告诉你。	"My dear niece, I have a few final words of advice for you."

This will greatly increase your base of operations.\nLook for people who have the <sprite name=""ui pip social expansion""> building expansion icon, they're actively looking for an opportunity.\nI added a new mission on your list as a reminder."
tut.lesson-16.3	第二，继续<b>扩张</b>领地与人脉。\n这座城市存在许多机会，但机会只留给那些声名显赫的人。	"Second, keep <b>expanding</b> both your territory and your connections.\nThere are many opportunities here, but they only come to those who make their name known."
tut.lesson-16.4	第三，对于这个世界你仍有许多未知之事。尝试通过与人们的交流，学习新<b>技能</b>，了解更多未知<b>资源</b>。有一些资源确实能令你收益颇丰！	"Third, there is still a lot you don't know. As you talk to people, try to learn new <b>skills</b>, and try to find out more about <b>resources</b> that you haven't explored yet. Some of those can be really profitable!"
tut.lesson-16.5	第四，为<b>竞争</b>做好准备。随着时间的推移，你会遭遇许多警察、街角流氓和其他帮派。等他们出现时，我会再为你提供一些建议。	"Fourth, expect <b>competition</b>. Over time you will meet cops, corner hooligans, and other outfits. I'll be back with more advice when that happens."
tut.lesson-16.6	最后，注意自己的<b>财政状况</b>。金钱就是商业齿轮的润滑剂，如果你花光了积蓄，所有业务都会陷入停滞。	"Finally, keep an eye on your <b>finances</b>. Money is the grease in the gears of commerce, as they say, and if you ever run out, this whole operation will come to a grinding halt."
tut.lesson-16.7	祝你好运，孩子！没想到你的进步如此迅速。\n当出现特殊情况时，我会继续为你出谋划策，但除此之外，你都要自力更生了。\n祝你在这座大城市里收获快乐！\n\n<i>（主要教程到此结束。你可以在主菜单的设置中再次开启教程。）</i>	"Good luck, kid! Who knew you'd do so well so quickly.\nI'll be back with more advice if the situation calls for it, but otherwise you're on your own.\nHave fun in the big city!\n\n<i>(The main tutorial will now be turned off. You can turn it back on in settings in the main menu.)</i>"

SPECIAL_tut.lesson-11.4	你也可以选择多种资源，然后点击左侧的高亮按钮将它们标记为喜爱。你每次打开这个信息栏时，这些喜爱的资源都会出现。\n你可以通过点击右侧的高亮按钮来切换喜爱的物品。\n你可以亲自尝试一下。当你完成时，可用同一个左上按钮关闭信息栏，然后我们将继续。	"You can also select multiple resources, and then mark your selection as favorite by pressing the left highlighted button. These favorites will be remembered whenever you open this overlay.\nYou can then toggle your favorites by hitting the right highlighted button.\nFeel free to try it out. When you're done, close the overlay using the same top left button, and we'll continue."


tut.lesson-15.title	运送	Deliveries
tut.lesson-15.1	现在你已拥有第二位成员了，可以给他指派一些工作——比如卖掉产品，购买原料，或者照看门面。\n现在就来试试吧。只需几个步骤，你的新成员就能完成基本送货路线，将你的自酿啤酒送到口渴的客户手中。\n在最右侧，点击<b>运送</b>下的按钮，开始指派任务。	"Now that you have a second crew member, you can delegate some of your work - such as selling off your product, buying up ingredients, or visiting your fronts.\nLet's try that now. In a few steps, your new hire will be running a basic delivery route to get your homemade beer out to thirsty customers.\nOn the far right hand side, click the button under <b>Deliveries</b> to get started."
tut.lesson-15.2	首先，我们需要分配一些人手来负责该运送业务。点击<b>+</b>按钮。	"First, we'll need to assign someone to run this delivery. Click on the <b>+</b> button."
tut.lesson-15.3	现在选择<b>指派司机</b>以便将你的新卡车和团伙成员指派到这条路线上。注意不要将自己也加进去——毕竟你还有比运酒更重要的事情要做。	"Now select <b>Assign a driver</b> to put your new truck and crew member on this route. Be sure not to add yourself - you’ve got more important things to do than deliver booze, after all."
tut.lesson-15.4	太好了！现在我们将创建运送计划表。这是一个循序渐进的指令，它将告诉司机从安全屋取得啤酒，然后开车四处售卖。\n计划表当前为空，请点击<b>“添加下一步”</b>。	"Great! Now we will create a delivery schedule. This will be a step-by-step instruction telling the driver to pick up beer from the safehouse, and then drive around to sell it.\nThe schedule is currently empty, so click <b>Add Next Step</b>."
tut.lesson-15.5	在运送步骤中，我们需要像指派人员说明要做什么以及要去哪里。先让他们从仓库里拿些自酿啤酒。\n首先，将初始步骤更改为<b>“仓库接货”</b>。	"A delivery step needs to specify what to do and where to go. We'll start by having them pick up some homemade beer from storage.\nSo first, change the initial step to <b>Storage pick up</b>."
tut.lesson-15.6	你的安全屋是该行动的默认地点，但你需要选择他们需要取得的资源。\n请从列表中选择<b>“自酿啤酒”</b>。	"Your safehouse is the default location for this action, but you need to select which resource they need to pick up.\nSelect <b>Homemade Beer</b> from the list."
tut.lesson-15.7	"此时，我们可以准确指定要移交到车中的物品数量。当前选择为<b>“全部”</b>，这代表司机会满载送货，这正是我们想要的。\n另一种可能情况下，司机将带上特定数量物品（<b>“定量”</b>），或确保一定数量的剩余储存空间并取走其余物品（<b>“全部取走，余留”</b>），或将物品移入车中，直到装载一定数量（<b>“达到特定数量”</b>）的物品。\n现在，我们要把卡车装满自酿啤酒，所以请选择<b>“全部”</b>。\n我们已经选择定量，请点击下方最左侧按钮以<b><sprite name=""ui save step"">保存这一步骤，然后返回</b>运送计划表。"	"At this point we could specify exactly how much to transfer into the vehicle. The current selection is <b>All</b>, meaning to take as much as can fit, which is exactly what we want.\nOther possibilities are to pick a specific amount (<b>Quantity</b>), or to make sure that there is a certain amount remaining in storage and to take the rest (<b>All but</b>), or to move things into their vehicle until their vehicle contains a set amount (<b>Until</b>).\nFor now, let's just have them fill up the truck with homemade beer, so we'll keep the <b>All</b> selection.\nNow that we chose quantity, click on the left-most button below to <b><sprite name=""ui save step""> save this step and return</b> to the delivery schedule."
tut.lesson-15.8	我们已经完成了第一步，现在我们要让他们卖掉这些自酿啤酒。点击高亮的<b>添加下一步骤</b>按钮，然后确定这些商品的运送目的地。	"Now that we’ve got the first step, we should get them selling homemade beer. Click the highlighted <b>Add Next Step</b> button and then we’ll decide where to go with the merchandise."
tut.lesson-15.9	"将操作更改为<b>“出售资源”</b>，然后从资源列表中选择<b>“自酿啤酒”</b>。\n目的地列表也将更改，包括已知所有购买自酿啤酒的生意伙伴。同样，你可以选择是否卖出客户当前愿意购买的最高数量（<b>“全部”</b>），或者你也可以选择尝试卖出数量（<b>“定量”</b>）。现在，我们来试试尽可能多地卖给他们。\n一旦生意顺利进行，请点击中间的<b><sprite name=""ui save add new"">“保存并添加新步骤”</b>按钮——它将保存此步骤，然后立即创建一个重复步骤。"	"Change the action to <b>Sell resources</b> and then select <b>Homemade Beer</b> from the list of resources.\nThe list of destinations will also change, to include all businesses you know that buy homemade beer. Once again, you can pick whether to sell them the maximum that they'll buy right now (<b>All</b>) or you can choose a quantity to try and sell (<b>Quantity</b>). For now, we'll try to sell them as much as they'll take.\nOnce things look good, click on the middle <b><sprite name=""ui save add new""> save and add new step</b> button - it will save this step and then immediately create a duplicate one."
tut.lesson-15.10	"现在看这里——通过点击“保存并添加”，你会得到和先前设置相同的新步骤。当然，我们并不想再卖给同一个人，所以我们来改变目的地。\n在地图上，你将看到每个想要购买自酿啤酒的商家。你可以简单地通过点击其中一个商家来选择新的目的地。\n快试试吧！这是一个很好的方式，可以快速地为你的运送添加许多步骤，并确保你的司机按照合理的路线进行运送。<b>一旦你完成设置，点击<sprite name=""ui save add new"">按钮来增加一个新的步骤</b>。"	"Now look at that - by clicking on ""save and add"", you’ve got a new step that has the same setup as the previous one. Of course we don’t want to sell to the same business again, so let’s change the destination.\nOn the map, you’ll see that every business that wants to buy your homemade beer is being shown. You can select a new destination for this step by simply clicking on one of the businesses.\nGo ahead and give it a try! This is a great way to add a lot of steps to your deliveries very quickly and make sure that your drivers are following a sensible route. <b>Once you’re done, click the <sprite name=""ui save add new""> button to add a new step</b>. "
tut.lesson-15.11	"你的司机卖掉所有啤酒后，手头会有很多现金，你可能不想让他们带着这些钱开车到处跑。所以我们得增加一个步骤，将现金放回安全屋。\n在刚刚创建的新步骤中，将操作命令更改为<b>“寄存”</b>，然后将资源更改为<b>“现金”</b>。\n“寄存”完成后，单击最左侧的<b><sprite name=""ui save step"">“保存并返回”</b>按钮，保存该步骤，并返回路线概览。"	"Your driver is going to have a lot of cash on hand after selling all that beer, and you probably don't want them driving around with it. So let's add a step to drop the cash off back at your safehouse.\nIn the new step you just created, change the action to <b>Storage drop off</b>, then change the resource to <b>Cash</b>.\nOnce you're done with that, click on the leftmost <b><sprite name=""ui save step""> save and return button</b> to save the step and return to the route overview."
tut.lesson-15.12	"大功告成——一条简易送货路线已经布置完毕！现在你一按下<b><sprite name=""ui start job"">“开始”按钮</b>，他们就会在下一回合立即开始工作，而且会一步步按照你的指示行动。\n一旦你的司机完成清单上的最后一步，他们就会重新开始。他们会一直按此行动，直到你让他们停下。请记住，你的司机在完成所分配的任务时，会耗尽其移动点数和行动点数，所以请一定选择合适人选，并明智地使用他们的经验升级。\n现在点击<b><sprite name=""ui start job"">“开始”按钮</b>开始工作。"	"There you have it - a simple delivery route! And now as soon as you hit the <b><sprite name=""ui start job""> start button</b>, they'll start the job on the next turn, and they will follow your instructions step by step.\nThen once your driver reaches the last step on the list, they will start over again, and they'll keep doing this until you tell them to stop. Just please remember that your driver will use up their movement points and action points as they go about doing their assigned tasks, so make sure to choose the right person for the job and use their experience upgrades wisely.\nNow click the <b><sprite name=""ui start job""> start button</b> to get going."
tut.lesson-15.13	"现在让我来分享一些关于运送路线的小提示。\n你可以点击路线名称以重新为其命名。这或许能帮你追踪路线。\n你可以点击步骤序号，输入新的位置，或者点击向上或下箭头，改变运送步骤顺序。\n最后，你还可以<sprite name=""ui mute step"">选择是否锁定单个步骤，并<sprite name=""ui start here"">强制让司机在下一回合中优先进行这一步骤。"	"Let me share a few other tips about your delivery routes.\nYou can rename routes by clicking on the name. That might help to keep track of them.\nYou can change the order of delivery steps, by clicking on the step number and entering a new position, as well as clicking their up and down arrows.\nFinally, you can <sprite name=""ui mute step""> toggle if an individual step is enabled or not, and <sprite name=""ui start here""> force a driver to a specific step on the next turn."
tut.lesson-15.14	我想你已经掌握了为团伙成员安排运送工作的基础知识。我们开始让司机卖出自酿啤酒，但是你也可以让他们运送其它你所需要的资源。\n为什么不加上一些步骤，让他们购买粗陶罐呢？确保添加一个步骤以获取一些现金，在你知道可以购买粗陶罐的商家添加两到三个站点，然后将它们带回你的安全屋。	"So, I think you’ve got the basics of organizing a delivery job for a crew member. We got them set up to sell homemade beer, but you can have them pick up the resources that you need to make it as well.\nSo why don’t you add a few more steps to buy stoneware crocks? Make sure to add a step to pick up a bit of cash, add two or three stops at businesses where you know that you can buy some crocks and then drop them off back at your safehouse."
tut.lesson-15.15	一旦你完成了操作，你的团伙成员就会自行维持自酿啤酒业务的运营。你就可以去做其它事情了——探索城市，结识新朋友，进行任务，学习技能，扩张领地——嗯，一位老板可是有很多事情要做的，不是吗？	"Once you’ve got that going, your crew will keep the homemade beer operation humming. This will free you up to do other things - exploring the city, meeting new people, working on missions, learning skills, expanding territory - well, there’s just so much for a boss to do these days, isn’t there?"

pedia.cat.gambling	赌博	Gambling
info.english-gin.name	英式金酒	English Gin
info.english-gin.desc	"<b>地方特产，并不是所有城市都有。</b>\n\n清澈、辛辣并带有刺柏浆果风味是它的最大特色。这一英格兰的进口货在伦敦“金酒风潮”时期曾经如此遍地开花，以至于其每品脱单价比啤酒还要便宜。现如今，进口金酒和本地酿制的金酒并没有太大的不同，不过，单单是这个故事本身就足以使它成为热门进口货，令高端夜总会和酒店不惜为之花费重金。\n\n英国人对其金酒的讲究程度无以复加，更使这种酒闻名遐迩。从伦敦干味金酒到普利茅斯式金酒，真可谓五花八门。因此，如果你有意向本地忠实客户销售这种酒，就一定要找些英国本地人帮忙，远渡重洋将金酒走私过来。\n\n<indent=10><b>基础价格\n<sprite name=""ui sell""> $90/箱</b></indent>\n\n<indent=20><i>生活就像是一粒药丸，不经润色任谁也无法下咽。</i></indent>\n<indent=40>—塞缪尔·约翰逊</indent>"	"<b>Regional product not available in all cities.</b>\n\nClean, sharp, and flavored with juniper berries, this import from England was once so ubiquitous during London's ""Gin Craze"" that it was cheaper per pint than beer. Nowadays its not too different from the stuff you could make locally, but the story alone makes this a hot import that high-end supper clubs and hotels will pay a pretty penny to acquire.\n\nThe English have any number of particularities about their Gin that makes it so famous, from London Dry to Plymouth style gins, so you'll have to smuggle these overseas with the help of some native Brits if you're interested in peddling it to your loyal customers in town.\n\n<indent=10><b>Base price\n<sprite name=""ui sell""> $90 per case</b></indent>\n\n<indent=20><i>Life is a pill which none of us can bear to swallow without gilding</i></indent>\n<indent=40>—Samuel Johnson</indent>"
info.scotch-whiskey.name	苏格兰威士忌	Scotch Whiskey
